157:     if wh is None:
158:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("warehouse_not_found")
159:     sl = await session.get(Seller, seller_id)
160:     if sl is None or sl.tenant_id != tenant_id:
161:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("seller_not_found")
162:     if marketplace == "wb" and wb_mp_warehouse_id is not None:
163:         mpw = await get_cached_mp_warehouse(session, tenant_id, wb_mp_warehouse_id)
164:         if mpw is None:
165:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_unknown")
166:     req = MarketplaceUnloadRequest(
167:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
168:         warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
169:         seller_id=seller_id,
170:         marketplace=marketplace,
171:         wb_mp_warehouse_id=wb_mp_warehouse_id,
172:         status=STATUS_DRAFT,
173:         ff_modified=False,
174:     )
175:     session.add(req)
176:     await assign_document_number_if_missing(session, tenant_id, DOC_TYPE_UNLOAD, req)
177:     await assign_display_number_if_missing(session, tenant_id, DOC_TYPE_UNLOAD, req)
178:     await session.commit()
179:     await session.refresh(req)
180:     from app.services.notification_trigger_service import notify_ff_marketplace_unload_created
181: 
182:     await notify_ff_marketplace_unload_created(session, req)
183:     await session.commit()
184:     return req
185: 
186: 
187: FF_DATE_EDITABLE_STATUSES = (STATUS_DRAFT, STATUS_SUBMITTED, STATUS_CONFIRMED)
188: 
189: 
190: async def set_wb_mp_warehouse(
191:     session: AsyncSession,
192:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
193:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
194:     *,
195:     wb_mp_warehouse_id: int,
196: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
197:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
198:     if req is None:
199:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
200:     if req.status not in SELLER_EDITABLE_STATUSES:
201:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_editable")
202:     if req.marketplace != "wb":
203:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_not_supported")
204:     mpw = await get_cached_mp_warehouse(session, tenant_id, wb_mp_warehouse_id)
205:     if mpw is None:
206:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_unknown")
207:     req.wb_mp_warehouse_id = wb_mp_warehouse_id
208:     await session.commit()
209:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
210:     assert r2 is not None
211:     return r2
212: 
213: 
214: async def patch_request(
215:     session: AsyncSession,
216:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
217:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
218:     *,
219:     user: User,
220:     wb_mp_warehouse_id: int | None = None,
221:     planned_shipment_date: date | None = None,
222:     set_planned_shipment_date: bool = False,
223: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
224:     from app.core.roles import FULFILLMENT_ADMIN, FULFILLMENT_SELLER
225: 
226:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
227:     if req is None:
228:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
229:     assert_request_visible(user, req)
230: 
231:     if wb_mp_warehouse_id is not None:
232:         if req.marketplace != "wb":
233:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_not_supported")
234:         if req.status not in SELLER_EDITABLE_STATUSES:
235:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_editable")
236:         if user.role == FULFILLMENT_SELLER and req.status != STATUS_DRAFT:
237:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_editable")
238:         mpw = await get_cached_mp_warehouse(session, tenant_id, wb_mp_warehouse_id)
239:         if mpw is None:
240:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_unknown")
241:         req.wb_mp_warehouse_id = wb_mp_warehouse_id
242: 
243:     if set_planned_shipment_date:
244:         if user.role == FULFILLMENT_SELLER:
245:             if req.status not in SELLER_EDITABLE_STATUSES:
246:                 raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_editable")
247:         elif user.role == FULFILLMENT_ADMIN:
248:             if req.status not in FF_DATE_EDITABLE_STATUSES:
249:                 raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_editable")
250:         else:
251:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("forbidden")
252:         req.planned_shipment_date = planned_shipment_date
253: 
254:     await session.commit()
255:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
256:     assert r2 is not None
257:     return r2
258: 
259: 
260: async def get_request(
261:     session: AsyncSession,
262:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
263:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
264: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest | None:
265:     stmt = (
266:         select(MarketplaceUnloadRequest)
267:         .where(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.id == request_id)
268:         .where(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.tenant_id == tenant_id)
269:         .options(
270:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.warehouse),
271:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.seller),
272:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.lines).selectinload(
273:                 MarketplaceUnloadLine.product
274:             ),
275:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.lines).selectinload(
276:                 MarketplaceUnloadLine.reservation
277:             ),
278:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.boxes)
279:             .selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadBox.lines)
280:             .selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadBoxLine.product),
281:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.boxes).selectinload(
282:                 MarketplaceUnloadBox.warehouse_box
283:             ),
284:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.pick_allocations).selectinload(
285:                 MarketplaceUnloadPickAllocation.product
286:             ),
287:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.pick_allocations).selectinload(
288:                 MarketplaceUnloadPickAllocation.storage_location
289:             ),
290:         )
291:     )
292:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
293:     return res.scalar_one_or_none()
294: 
295: 
296: async def list_requests(
297:     session: AsyncSession,
298:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
299:     *,
300:     seller_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
301: ) -> list[MarketplaceUnloadRequest]:
302:     stmt = (
303:         select(MarketplaceUnloadRequest)
304:         .where(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.tenant_id == tenant_id)
305:         .options(
306:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.warehouse),
307:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.seller),
308:             selectinload(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.lines),
309:         )
310:         .order_by(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.created_at.desc())
311:     )
312:     if seller_id is not None:
313:         stmt = stmt.where(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.seller_id == seller_id)
314:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
315:     return list(res.scalars().unique().all())
316: 
317: 
318: async def _outbound_reserved_by_product(
319:     session: AsyncSession,
320:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
321:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
322:     product_ids: list[uuid.UUID],
323: ) -> dict[uuid.UUID, int]:
324:     if not product_ids:
325:         return {}
326:     stmt = (
327:         select(
328:             InventoryReservation.product_id,
329:             func.coalesce(func.sum(InventoryReservation.quantity), 0),
330:         )
331:         .join(
332:             OutboundShipmentLine,
333:             OutboundShipmentLine.id == InventoryReservation.outbound_shipment_line_id,
334:         )
335:         .join(
336:             OutboundShipmentRequest,
337:             OutboundShipmentRequest.id == OutboundShipmentLine.request_id,
338:         )
339:         .outerjoin(
340:             StorageLocation,
341:             StorageLocation.id == InventoryReservation.storage_location_id,
342:         )
343:         .where(
344:             InventoryReservation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
345:             InventoryReservation.product_id.in_(product_ids),
346:             OutboundShipmentRequest.status.in_(inventory_service.OUTBOUND_RESERVE_STATUSES),
347:             or_(
348:                 and_(
349:                     InventoryReservation.storage_location_id.isnot(None),
350:                     StorageLocation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
351:                     StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
352:                 ),
353:                 and_(
354:                     InventoryReservation.storage_location_id.is_(None),
355:                     InventoryReservation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
356:                 ),
357:             ),
358:         )
359:         .group_by(InventoryReservation.product_id)
360:     )
361:     result = await session.execute(stmt)
362:     return {product_id: int(quantity or 0) for product_id, quantity in result.all()}
363: 
364: 
365: async def _mp_reserved_by_product(
366:     session: AsyncSession,
367:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
368:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
369:     product_ids: list[uuid.UUID],
370:     *,
371:     exclude_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
372: ) -> dict[uuid.UUID, int]:
373:     if not product_ids:
374:         return {}
375:     stmt = (
376:         select(
377:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.product_id,
378:             func.coalesce(func.sum(MarketplaceUnloadReservation.quantity), 0),
379:         )
380:         .join(
381:             MarketplaceUnloadLine,
382:             MarketplaceUnloadLine.id == MarketplaceUnloadReservation.marketplace_unload_line_id,
383:         )
384:         .join(
385:             MarketplaceUnloadRequest,
386:             MarketplaceUnloadRequest.id == MarketplaceUnloadLine.request_id,
387:         )
388:         .where(
389:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
390:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
391:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.product_id.in_(product_ids),
392:             MarketplaceUnloadRequest.status.in_(RESERVE_STATUSES),
393:         )
394:         .group_by(MarketplaceUnloadReservation.product_id)
395:     )
396:     if exclude_request_id is not None:
397:         stmt = stmt.where(MarketplaceUnloadRequest.id != exclude_request_id)
398:     result = await session.execute(stmt)
399:     return {product_id: int(quantity or 0) for product_id, quantity in result.all()}
400: 
401: 
402: async def _mp_reserved_qty_for_product(
403:     session: AsyncSession,
404:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
405:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
406:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
407:     *,
408:     exclude_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
409: ) -> int:
410:     reserved = await _mp_reserved_by_product(
411:         session,
412:         tenant_id,
413:         warehouse_id,
414:         [product_id],
415:         exclude_request_id=exclude_request_id,
416:     )
417:     return int(reserved.get(product_id, 0))

420: async def _available_product_qty_in_warehouse(
421:     session: AsyncSession,
422:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
423:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
424:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
425:     *,
426:     exclude_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
427: ) -> int:
428:     availability = await _available_product_availability_in_warehouse(
429:         session,
430:         tenant_id,
431:         warehouse_id,
432:         product_id,
433:         exclude_request_id=exclude_request_id,
434:     )
435:     return availability.available
436: 
437: 
438: async def _available_product_availability_in_warehouse(
439:     session: AsyncSession,
440:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
441:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
442:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
443:     *,
444:     exclude_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
445: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadAvailability:
446:     on_hand = await inventory_service.storage_on_hand_in_warehouse(
447:         session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, product_id
448:     )
449:     sorting_on_hand = await inventory_service.sorting_on_hand_in_warehouse(
450:         session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, product_id
451:     )
452:     reserved_outbound = (
453:         await _outbound_reserved_by_product(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, [product_id])
454:     ).get(product_id, 0)
455:     reserved_mp = await _mp_reserved_qty_for_product(
456:         session,
457:         tenant_id,
458:         warehouse_id,
459:         product_id,
460:         exclude_request_id=exclude_request_id,
461:     )
462:     from app.services.fbs_stock_availability_service import (
463:         fbs_reserved_qty_for_product,
464:     )
465: 
466:     reserved_fbs = await fbs_reserved_qty_for_product(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, product_id)
467:     directions = await stock_direction_service.direction_totals_by_product(
468:         session, tenant_id, [product_id]
469:     )
470:     direction_total = directions.get(product_id)
471:     if direction_total is not None and direction_total.has_any:
472:         # Existing order reservations protect real stock in every publication mode.
473:         return MarketplaceUnloadAvailability(
474:             available=on_hand
475:             + sorting_on_hand
476:             - direction_total.total
477:             - reserved_outbound
478:             - reserved_mp
479:             - reserved_fbs,
480:             uses_free_fbo_pool=True,
481:         )
482:     return MarketplaceUnloadAvailability(
483:         available=(
484:             on_hand
485:             + sorting_on_hand
486:             - reserved_outbound
487:             - reserved_mp
488:             - reserved_fbs
489:         ),
490:         uses_free_fbo_pool=False,
491:     )
492: 
493: 
494: async def _assert_available_for_unload_quantity(
495:     session: AsyncSession,
496:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
497:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
498:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
499:     quantity: int,
500:     *,
501:     exclude_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
502:     product_name: str | None = None,
503:     sku_code: str | None = None,
504: ) -> None:
505:     await inventory_service.lock_stock_product(session, tenant_id, product_id)
506:     availability = await _available_product_availability_in_warehouse(
507:         session,
508:         tenant_id,
509:         warehouse_id,
510:         product_id,
511:         exclude_request_id=exclude_request_id,
512:     )
513:     if availability.available >= quantity:
514:         return
515:     code = "insufficient_free_fbo" if availability.uses_free_fbo_pool else "insufficient_available"
516:     product_label = product_name or sku_code or str(product_id)
517:     suffix = f" ({sku_code})" if sku_code and sku_code != product_name else ""
518:     prefix = (
519:         "Недостаточно свободного FBO остатка"
520:         if availability.uses_free_fbo_pool
521:         else "Недостаточно доступного остатка"
522:     )
523:     raise MarketplaceUnloadError(
524:         code,
525:         {
526:             "code": code,
527:             "product_id": str(product_id),
528:             "product_name": product_name,
529:             "sku_code": sku_code,
530:             "available": availability.available,
531:             "attempted": quantity,
532:             "message": (
533:                 f"{prefix}: {product_label}{suffix}. "
534:                 f"Доступно {availability.available} шт, пытаются {quantity} шт."
535:             ),
536:         },
537:     )
538: 
539: 
540: async def list_available_products(
541:     session: AsyncSession,
542:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
543:     *,
544:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
545:     seller_id: uuid.UUID,
546:     exclude_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
547: ) -> list[MarketplaceUnloadAvailableProduct]:
548:     """Readonly MP availability: storage + sorting - outbound - other MP reserves."""
549:     warehouse = await get_warehouse(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
550:     if warehouse is None:
551:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("warehouse_not_found")
552:     seller = await session.get(Seller, seller_id)
553:     if seller is None or seller.tenant_id != tenant_id:
554:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("seller_not_found")
555:     if exclude_request_id is not None:
556:         request = await get_request(session, tenant_id, exclude_request_id)
557:         if (
558:             request is None
559:             or request.warehouse_id != warehouse_id
560:             or request.seller_id != seller_id
561:         ):
562:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
563: 
564:     stock_stmt = (
565:         select(
566:             Product.id,
567:             Product.sku_code,
568:             Product.name,
569:             func.coalesce(func.sum(InventoryBalance.quantity), 0),
570:         )
571:         .join(InventoryBalance, InventoryBalance.product_id == Product.id)
572:         .join(StorageLocation, StorageLocation.id == InventoryBalance.storage_location_id)
573:         .where(
574:             Product.tenant_id == tenant_id,
575:             Product.seller_id == seller_id,
576:             InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
577:             StorageLocation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
578:             StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
579:         )
580:         .group_by(Product.id, Product.sku_code, Product.name)
581:         .order_by(Product.sku_code)
582:     )
583:     stock_rows = (await session.execute(stock_stmt)).all()
584:     product_ids = [product_id for product_id, *_ in stock_rows]
585:     outbound_reserved = await _outbound_reserved_by_product(
586:         session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, product_ids
587:     )
588:     mp_reserved = await _mp_reserved_by_product(
589:         session,
590:         tenant_id,
591:         warehouse_id,
592:         product_ids,
593:         exclude_request_id=exclude_request_id,
594:     )
595:     from app.services.fbs_stock_availability_service import (
596:         fbs_reserved_by_product,
597:     )
598: 
599:     fbs_reserved = await fbs_reserved_by_product(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, product_ids)
600:     direction_totals = await stock_direction_service.direction_totals_by_product(
601:         session, tenant_id, product_ids
602:     )
603:     return [
604:         MarketplaceUnloadAvailableProduct(
605:             product_id=product_id,
606:             sku_code=sku_code,
607:             product_name=product_name,
608:             available=max(
609:                 0,
610:                 quantity_total
611:                 - (
612:                     direction_totals[product_id].total
613:                     if direction_totals.get(product_id) is not None
614:                     and direction_totals[product_id].has_any
615:                     else 0
616:                 )
617:                 - fbs_reserved.get(product_id, 0)
618:                 - outbound_reserved.get(product_id, 0)
619:                 - mp_reserved.get(product_id, 0),
620:             ),
621:         )
622:         for product_id, sku_code, product_name, quantity_total in stock_rows
623:     ]
624: 
625: 
626: async def _schedule_fbs_publish_for_request(session: AsyncSession, request_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
627:     """Резерв под отгрузку на МП вычитается из того же пула, что и ФБС.
628: 
629:     Значит любое изменение этого резерва меняет цифру, которую WB должен увидеть
630:     по товарам селлера, — ставим публикацию в очередь.
631:     """
632:     req = await session.get(MarketplaceUnloadRequest, request_id)
633:     if req is not None:
634:         schedule_seller_stock_publish(session, req.tenant_id, req.seller_id)
635: 
636: 
637: async def _release_reservations(session: AsyncSession, request_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
638:     line_ids_stmt = select(MarketplaceUnloadLine.id).where(
639:         MarketplaceUnloadLine.request_id == request_id
640:     )
641:     res = await session.execute(line_ids_stmt)
642:     line_ids = [row[0] for row in res.all()]
643:     if not line_ids:
644:         return
645:     await session.execute(
646:         delete(MarketplaceUnloadReservation).where(
647:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.marketplace_unload_line_id.in_(line_ids)
648:         )
649:     )
650:     await _schedule_fbs_publish_for_request(session, request_id)
651: 
652: 
653: async def _apply_reservations(session: AsyncSession, req: MarketplaceUnloadRequest) -> None:
654:     await _release_reservations(session, req.id)
655:     for ln in req.lines:
656:         session.add(
657:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation(
658:                 tenant_id=req.tenant_id,
659:                 marketplace_unload_line_id=ln.id,
660:                 product_id=ln.product_id,
661:                 warehouse_id=req.warehouse_id,
662:                 quantity=int(ln.quantity),
663:             )
664:         )
665:     schedule_seller_stock_publish(session, req.tenant_id, req.seller_id)
666: 
667: 
668: async def add_line(
669:     session: AsyncSession,
670:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
671:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
672:     *,
673:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
674:     quantity: int,
675:     allow_ff_confirmed: bool = False,
676: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadLine:
677:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
678:     if req is None:
679:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
680:     editable = SELLER_EDITABLE_STATUSES if not allow_ff_confirmed else FF_LINE_EDITABLE_STATUSES
681:     if req.status not in editable:
682:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_editable")
683:     prod = await session.get(Product, product_id)
684:     if prod is None or prod.tenant_id != tenant_id:
685:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("product_not_found")
686:     if req.seller_id is not None and prod.seller_id != req.seller_id:
687:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("product_seller_mismatch")
688:     await _assert_available_for_unload_quantity(
689:         session,
690:         tenant_id,
691:         req.warehouse_id,
692:         product_id,
693:         quantity,
694:         exclude_request_id=req.id if req.status in RESERVE_STATUSES else None,
695:         product_name=prod.name,
696:         sku_code=prod.sku_code,
697:     )
698:     line = MarketplaceUnloadLine(
699:         request_id=req.id,
700:         product_id=product_id,
701:         quantity=quantity,
702:     )
703:     session.add(line)
704:     try:
705:         await session.flush()
706:     except IntegrityError:
707:         await session.rollback()
708:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("duplicate_line") from None
709:     if allow_ff_confirmed and req.status == STATUS_CONFIRMED:
710:         req.ff_modified = True
711:     await session.commit()
712:     await session.refresh(line, attribute_names=["product"])
713:     await _sync_packaging_task_for_unload(session, tenant_id, request_id)
714:     return line
715: 
716: 
717: async def replace_lines(
718:     session: AsyncSession,
719:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
720:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
721:     *,
722:     lines: list[tuple[uuid.UUID, int]],
723: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
724:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
725:     if req is None:
726:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
727:     if req.status != STATUS_DRAFT:
728:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_editable")
729: 
730:     normalized: dict[uuid.UUID, int] = {}
731:     for product_id, qty in lines:
732:         if qty < 1:
733:             continue
734:         normalized[product_id] = normalized.get(product_id, 0) + qty
735: 
736:     products: dict[uuid.UUID, Product] = {}
737:     for product_id in normalized:
738:         prod = await session.get(Product, product_id)
739:         if prod is None or prod.tenant_id != tenant_id:
740:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("product_not_found")
741:         if req.seller_id is not None and prod.seller_id != req.seller_id:
742:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("product_seller_mismatch")
743:         products[product_id] = prod
744: 
745:     for product_id, qty in normalized.items():
746:         prod = products[product_id]
747:         await _assert_available_for_unload_quantity(
748:             session,
749:             tenant_id,
750:             req.warehouse_id,
751:             product_id,
752:             qty,
753:             product_name=prod.name,
754:             sku_code=prod.sku_code,
755:         )
756: 
757:     for ln in list(req.lines):
758:         await session.delete(ln)
759:     await session.flush()
760: 
761:     for product_id, qty in normalized.items():
762:         session.add(
763:             MarketplaceUnloadLine(
764:                 request_id=req.id,
765:                 product_id=product_id,
766:                 quantity=qty,
767:             )
768:         )
769:     await session.commit()
770:     await _sync_packaging_task_for_unload(session, tenant_id, request_id)
771:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
772:     assert r2 is not None
773:     return r2
774: 
775: 
776: async def plan_request(
777:     session: AsyncSession,
778:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
779:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
780: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
781:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
782:     if req is None:
783:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
784:     if req.status != STATUS_DRAFT:
785:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("bad_status")
786:     if req.marketplace == "wb" and req.wb_mp_warehouse_id is None:
787:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_required")
788:     if not req.lines:
789:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("no_lines")
790:     if req.planned_shipment_date is None:
791:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("planned_shipment_date_required")
792:     if req.marketplace == "wb":
793:         assert req.wb_mp_warehouse_id is not None
794:         mpw = await get_cached_mp_warehouse(session, tenant_id, int(req.wb_mp_warehouse_id))
795:         if mpw is None:
796:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_unknown")
797:     for ln in req.lines:
798:         await _assert_available_for_unload_quantity(
799:             session,
800:             tenant_id,
801:             req.warehouse_id,
802:             ln.product_id,
803:             ln.quantity,
804:             exclude_request_id=req.id,
805:         )
806:     await _apply_reservations(session, req)
807:     req.status = STATUS_SUBMITTED
808:     await session.commit()
809:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
810:     assert r2 is not None
811:     return r2
812: 
813: 
814: async def unplan_request(
815:     session: AsyncSession,
816:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
817:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
818: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
819:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
820:     if req is None:
821:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
822:     if req.status != STATUS_SUBMITTED:
823:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("bad_status")
824:     await _release_reservations(session, req.id)
825:     req.status = STATUS_DRAFT
826:     await session.commit()
827:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
828:     assert r2 is not None
829:     return r2
830: 
831: 
832: async def confirm_request(
833:     session: AsyncSession,
834:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
835:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
836:     *,
837:     planned_shipment_date: date | None = None,
838: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
839:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
840:     if req is None:
841:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
842:     if req.status not in (STATUS_DRAFT, STATUS_SUBMITTED):
843:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("bad_status")
844:     if req.marketplace == "wb" and req.wb_mp_warehouse_id is None:
845:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_required")
846:     if not req.lines:
847:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("no_lines")
848:     if req.marketplace == "wb":
849:         assert req.wb_mp_warehouse_id is not None
850:         mpw = await get_cached_mp_warehouse(session, tenant_id, int(req.wb_mp_warehouse_id))
851:         if mpw is None:
852:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_unknown")
853:     effective_date = (
854:         planned_shipment_date if planned_shipment_date is not None else req.planned_shipment_date
855:     )
856:     if effective_date is None:
857:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("planned_shipment_date_required")
858:     if req.status == STATUS_DRAFT:
859:         for ln in req.lines:
860:             await _assert_available_for_unload_quantity(
861:                 session,
862:                 tenant_id,
863:                 req.warehouse_id,
864:                 ln.product_id,
865:                 ln.quantity,
866:                 exclude_request_id=req.id,
867:             )
868:         await _apply_reservations(session, req)
869:     req.planned_shipment_date = effective_date
870:     req.status = STATUS_CONFIRMED
871:     await session.commit()
872: 
873:     from app.services import packaging_task_service as pkg_svc
874: 
875:     await pkg_svc.ensure_task_for_unload(session, tenant_id, request_id)
876: 
877:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
878:     assert r2 is not None
879:     return r2
880: 
881: 
882: async def submit_request(
883:     session: AsyncSession,
884:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
885:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
886: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
887:     """Legacy alias: FF confirms from draft or submitted."""
888:     return await confirm_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
889: 
890: 
891: async def release_reservations_for_shipped(session: AsyncSession, request_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
892:     await _release_reservations(session, request_id)
893: 
894: 
895: async def reduce_reservation_for_collect(
896:     session: AsyncSession,
897:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
898:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
899:     quantity: int,
900: ) -> None:
901:     """DEC-016: collect_into_box reduces warehouse reserve alongside on_hand deduct."""
902:     if quantity < 1:
903:         return
904:     stmt = (
905:         select(MarketplaceUnloadReservation)
906:         .join(
907:             MarketplaceUnloadLine,
908:             MarketplaceUnloadLine.id == MarketplaceUnloadReservation.marketplace_unload_line_id,
909:         )
910:         .where(
911:             MarketplaceUnloadLine.request_id == request_id,
912:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.product_id == product_id,
913:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.quantity > 0,
914:         )
915:         .with_for_update()
916:     )
917:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
918:     remaining = quantity
919:     for reservation in res.scalars().all():
920:         if remaining < 1:
921:             break
922:         take = min(int(reservation.quantity), remaining)
923:         reservation.quantity = int(reservation.quantity) - take
924:         remaining -= take
925: 
926: 
927: async def restore_reservation_for_remove(
928:     session: AsyncSession,
929:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
930:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
931:     quantity: int,
932:     *,
933:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
934:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
935: ) -> None:
936:     """DEC-016: remove-from-box restores warehouse reserve."""
937:     if quantity < 1:
938:         return
939:     line_stmt = select(MarketplaceUnloadLine).where(
940:         MarketplaceUnloadLine.request_id == request_id,
941:         MarketplaceUnloadLine.product_id == product_id,
942:     )
943:     line_res = await session.execute(line_stmt)
944:     unload_line = line_res.scalar_one_or_none()
945:     if unload_line is None:
946:         return
947:     res_stmt = (
948:         select(MarketplaceUnloadReservation)
949:         .where(
950:             MarketplaceUnloadReservation.marketplace_unload_line_id == unload_line.id,
951:         )
952:         .with_for_update()
953:     )
954:     res_row = await session.execute(res_stmt)
955:     reservation = res_row.scalar_one_or_none()
956:     if reservation is None:
957:         reservation = MarketplaceUnloadReservation(
958:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
959:             marketplace_unload_line_id=unload_line.id,
960:             product_id=product_id,
961:             warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
962:             quantity=0,
963:         )
964:         session.add(reservation)
965:         await session.flush()
966:     reservation.quantity = int(reservation.quantity) + quantity
967: 
968: 
969: async def delete_empty_boxes_for_ship(session: AsyncSession, req: MarketplaceUnloadRequest) -> None:
970:     """DEC-002: empty boxes (no lines) are removed when shipment is posted."""
971:     for box in list(req.boxes):
972:         if not box.lines:
973:             await session.delete(box)
974: 
975: 
976: def distributed_qty_by_product(req: MarketplaceUnloadRequest) -> dict[uuid.UUID, int]:
977:     """Fact qty per product from shipment box lines."""
978:     picked: dict[uuid.UUID, int] = {}
979:     for b in req.boxes:
980:         for bl in b.lines:
981:             picked[bl.product_id] = picked.get(bl.product_id, 0) + int(bl.quantity)
982:     return picked
983: 
984: 
985: def compute_has_discrepancy(req: MarketplaceUnloadRequest) -> bool:
986:     """Plan (request lines) vs fact (box quantities), mirroring inbound complete_verification."""
987:     distributed = distributed_qty_by_product(req)
988:     return any(distributed.get(ln.product_id, 0) != int(ln.quantity) for ln in req.lines)
989: 
990: 
991: def has_incomplete_distribution(req: MarketplaceUnloadRequest) -> bool:
992:     """True when nothing collected or any line plan ≠ fact."""
993:     distributed = distributed_qty_by_product(req)
994:     if not distributed:
995:         return True
996:     return compute_has_discrepancy(req)
997: 
998: 
999: async def complete_unload(
1000:     session: AsyncSession,
1001:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1002:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
1003:     *,
1004:     acknowledge_discrepancy: bool = False,
1005:     performer_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
1006: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
1007:     """Single completion op: ship unload; set has_discrepancy when plan ≠ fact."""
1008:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
1009:     if req is None:
1010:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
1011:     if req.status not in EXECUTION_STATUSES:
1012:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("bad_status")
1013:     if not req.lines:
1014:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("no_lines")
1015:     if req.planned_shipment_date is None:
1016:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("planned_shipment_date_required")
1017:     if req.marketplace == "wb" and req.wb_mp_warehouse_id is None:
1018:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("wb_mp_warehouse_required")
1019: 
1020:     from app.services import packaging_task_service as pkg_svc
1021: 
1022:     await pkg_svc.assert_unload_marking_done(session, tenant_id, request_id)
1023: 
1024:     distributed = distributed_qty_by_product(req)
1025:     if not distributed or sum(distributed.values()) < 1:
1026:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("distribution_incomplete")
1027: 
1028:     has_discrepancy = compute_has_discrepancy(req)
1029:     if has_discrepancy:
1030:         if not acknowledge_discrepancy:
1031:             raise MarketplaceUnloadError("distribution_incomplete")
1032:         req.ff_modified = True
1033: 
1034:     # Отгрузка на маркетплейс завершается локально — одинаково для WB и Ozon.
1035:     #
1036:     # Здесь стоял вызов `dispatch_unload` через границу провайдера, заведённый
1037:     # 25.08.2026, когда кабинет Ozon отвечал 403 и проверить было нечем. За ним
1038:     # никогда не было метода Ozon: у Ozon это не «передача документа», а заявка
1039:     # на поставку на его склад — отдельная многошаговая схема из черновика
1040:     # (`/v1/draft/direct/create`), кластера, таймслота (`/v2/draft/timeslot/info`),
1041:     # пропуска на водителя и машину. Ни одного из этих полей у нашего документа
1042:     # нет: `MarketplaceUnloadRequest` знает только склад ФФ, селлера, дату и
1043:     # строки, а колонка `external_supply_id` в коде не заполняется нигде.
1044:     #
1045:     # Практическое следствие прежнего кода было хуже, чем отсутствие интеграции:
1046:     # локальный фейк отвечал «403, код 7», отгрузка Ozon не завершалась никогда,
1047:     # документ навсегда оставался в `collecting`. Товар физически уезжал, а
1048:     # списания и начисления не происходило. Причина при этом называлась ложная —
1049:     # «кабинет Ozon недоступен», хотя кабинет отвечает.
1050:     #
1051:     # У Wildberries эта же операция всегда завершалась локально: документ
1052:     # поставки на складе маркетплейса создаёт селлер в своём кабинете, а ФФ
1053:     # отвечает за физическую отгрузку. Для Ozon верно ровно то же самое.
1054: 
1055:     req.has_discrepancy = has_discrepancy
1056:     await delete_empty_boxes_for_ship(session, req)
1057:     req.status = STATUS_SHIPPED
1058:     occurred_at = datetime.now(UTC)
1059:     if req.shipped_at is None:
1060:         req.shipped_at = occurred_at
1061:     if req.completed_by_user_id is None:
1062:         req.completed_by_user_id = performer_id
1063:     await record_marketplace_unload(
1064:         session,
1065:         request=req,
1066:         distributed=distributed,
1067:         occurred_at=req.shipped_at,
1068:         performer_id=req.completed_by_user_id,
1069:     )
1070:     try:
1071:         for charged_service_code in ("marketplace_outbound", PACKING_SERVICE_CODE):
1072:             # Упаковку считаем по отгруженному товару, а не по событиям упаковки:
1073:             # уехавшая коробка упакована независимо от того, нажал ли оператор
1074:             # «всё упаковано». Штуки и построчный состав — те же, что у отгрузки.
1075:             await record_operational_charge(
1076:                 session,
1077:                 tenant_id=tenant_id,
1078:                 seller_id=req.seller_id,
1079:                 source_type="marketplace_unload",
1080:                 source_id=req.id,
1081:                 source="marketplace_unload",
1082:                 service_code=charged_service_code,
1083:                 quantity=Decimal(sum(distributed.values())),
1084:                 occurred_at=occurred_at,
1085:                 performer_id=performer_id,
1086:                 lines=product_billing_lines(
1087:                     (product_id, Decimal(quantity), {"marketplace_unload_request_id": str(req.id)})
1088:                     for product_id, quantity in distributed.items() if quantity
1089:                 ),
1090:             )
1091:     except BillingLedgerError:
1092:         if req.seller_id is not None:
1093:             await record_operational_billing_issue(
1094:                 session,
1095:                 tenant_id=tenant_id,
1096:                 seller_id=req.seller_id,
1097:                 occurred_at=occurred_at,
1098:             )
1099:     await release_reservations_for_shipped(session, req.id)
1100:     await session.commit()
1101:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
1102:     assert r2 is not None
1103:     return r2
1104:

1161: 
1162: async def cancel_request(
1163:     session: AsyncSession,
1164:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1165:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
1166:     *,
1167:     performer_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
1168: ) -> MarketplaceUnloadRequest:
1169:     """Cancel before shipment, or reverse shipped billing without changing warehouse fact."""
1170:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
1171:     if req is None:
1172:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
1173:     if req.status == STATUS_CANCELLED:
1174:         return req
1175:     if req.status not in CANCELLABLE_STATUSES + BILLING_REVERSIBLE_STATUSES:
1176:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("bad_status")
1177: 
1178:     if req.status in BILLING_REVERSIBLE_STATUSES:
1179:         occurred_at = datetime.now(UTC)
1180:         for reversed_service_code in ("marketplace_outbound", PACKING_SERVICE_CODE):
1181:             # Отменяем обе строки документа: и отгрузку, и упаковку по ней.
1182:             await record_operational_reversal(
1183:                 session,
1184:                 tenant_id=tenant_id,
1185:                 source_type="marketplace_unload",
1186:                 source_id=req.id,
1187:                 occurred_at=occurred_at,
1188:                 performer_id=performer_id,
1189:                 service_code=reversed_service_code,
1190:             )
1191:         if req.cancelled_by_user_id is None:
1192:             req.cancelled_by_user_id = performer_id
1193:         if req.cancelled_at is None:
1194:             req.cancelled_at = occurred_at
1195:         await record_marketplace_unload(
1196:             session,
1197:             request=req,
1198:             distributed=distributed_qty_by_product(req),
1199:             occurred_at=req.cancelled_at,
1200:             performer_id=req.cancelled_by_user_id,
1201:             reversal=True,
1202:         )
1203:     else:
1204:         from app.services import marketplace_unload_collect_service as collect_svc
1205: 
1206:         await collect_svc.rollback_all_collected_for_cancel(
1207:             session,
1208:             tenant_id,
1209:             req.warehouse_id,
1210:             request_id,
1211:             actor_user_id=performer_id,
1212:         )
1213:         await _release_reservations(session, request_id)
1214:         req.status = STATUS_CANCELLED
1215:     await session.commit()
1216:     r2 = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
1217:     assert r2 is not None
1218:     return r2
1219: 
1220: 
1221: async def delete_draft_request(
1222:     session: AsyncSession,
1223:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1224:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
1225: ) -> None:
1226:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
1227:     if req is None:
1228:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
1229:     if req.status != STATUS_DRAFT:
1230:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_draft")
1231:     await _release_reservations(session, request_id)
1232:     await session.delete(req)
1233:     await session.commit()
1234: 
1235: 
1236: async def delete_line(
1237:     session: AsyncSession,
1238:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1239:     request_id: uuid.UUID,
1240:     line_id: uuid.UUID,
1241: ) -> None:
1242:     req = await get_request(session, tenant_id, request_id)
1243:     if req is None:
1244:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_found")
1245:     if req.status not in DELETE_EDITABLE_STATUSES:
1246:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("not_draft")
1247:     line = await session.get(MarketplaceUnloadLine, line_id)
1248:     if line is None or line.request_id != request_id:
1249:         raise MarketplaceUnloadError("line_not_found")
1250:     await session.delete(line)
1251:     await session.commit()
1252:     await _sync_packaging_task_for_unload(session, tenant_id, request_id)

11: from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

13: from app.models.fbs_binding_stock_pool import FbsBindingStockPool
